1	# Wan2GP Fork Migration — Design Document
2	
3	## 1. Executive Summary
4	`reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` is currently a flat vendored snapshot with no `.git` directory, so it cannot be cleanly rebased onto `deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP` even though it is roughly 50 days and about 9 minor versions behind. The hottest drift zone is `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/`, where 22 files differ and the working diff is roughly 4303 lines.
5	
6	This migration plan is intentionally a planning artifact, not an implementation batch. It breaks the migration into 4 sprints of 2 weeks each so the team can inventory drift first, cut over to a maintainable fork-plus-patch model second, resync the risky `models/ltx2/` surface third, and finish with sustainability controls last.
7	
8	The non-negotiable constraint is that no functionality may be lost during any sprint. The plan therefore treats these as protected behaviors that must survive with explicit verification: IC-LoRA auto-injection for pose, depth, canny, and cameraman modes; the newly landed `ltx_anchor` kind; the existing `ltx_control` pixel cross-fade guide-video path; the `ltx_hybrid` route; `clear_conditioning` keyframe-token stripping before VAE decode; the existing 6-file travel test suite; and the import-path contract that supports both dotted `Wan2GP.*` callers and bare-module callers.
9	
10	## 2. Current-State Inventory
11	
12	### 2a. Vendored tree facts
13	`reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` is currently a plain copied tree rather than a repository checkout, so there is no `.git` metadata to rebase, cherry-pick, or diff-history against. The vendored changelog currently tops out at `WanGP v10.01` dated January 1, 2026, while upstream context for this plan is `v10.951` dated February 19, 2026.
14	
15	The hottest resync zone is `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/`, where the working comparison indicates 22 differing files and about 4303 lines of diff. That is large enough that the migration cannot safely be framed as a single blind replacement.
16	
17	The repo also has a non-trivial import and runtime surface already coupled to the current mount path. The approved background for this plan treats that as a 26+ call-site contract, which is why the migration must preserve both filesystem location and import semantics rather than just swapping in a dependency reference.
18	
19	### 2b. Genuinely missing upstream items
20	The current vendored tree is missing several upstream features that are desired outcomes of the migration rather than already-carried drift. Confirmed missing items include `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py` and `Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json`.
21	
22	Other upstream-side capabilities that this design document should plan to absorb during the migration include `_apply_gamma_to_media`, `LTX2_OUTPAINT_GAMMA`, `LTX2_DISABLE_STAGE2_WITH_CONTROL_VIDEO`, and the newer prompt-enhancer improvements. These belong in the migration inventory because they are feature gaps, not just cleanup opportunities.
23	
24	### 2c. Already-vendored drift-only items
25	`Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` is already vendored in the current tree. It should therefore be treated as a drift candidate that may need reconciliation with upstream head, not as a missing upstream backfill.
26	
27	That distinction matters for sprint design: Sprint 2 can absorb `self_refiner` drift as a low-risk resync task, while `res2s.py` and `ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` remain true additions.
28	
29	### 2d. Patch seam and lifecycle
30	The repo already has an established patch seam for Wan2GP-specific runtime behavior. The module-load trigger is `source/__init__.py:6`, which ensures `source.models.wgp.wgp_patches` is importable during bootstrap.
31	
32	Actual patch application happens later in the runtime lifecycle at `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266`, where `apply_all_wgp_patches(wgp, self.wan_root)` is called after the Wan2GP module is loaded and before orchestration proceeds. The migration plan should preserve this existing seam instead of introducing a second patch package.
33	
34	### 2e. banodoco-carry items
35	Some behaviors are not generic upstream drift and must be treated as load-bearing banodoco carry items until they are either upstreamed or deliberately relocated. The clearest examples are `clear_conditioning` in `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79`, the `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE` registry in `source/core/params/travel_guidance.py`, and the `ltx_anchor`-related orchestrator rewiring around `_build_segment_anchor_guidance_config`, PATH A skip behavior, and segment-local anchor remapping.
36	
37	These items are the reason the migration cannot be framed as “replace vendor tree with upstream head.” Each one needs an explicit carry-location decision later in the plan.
38	
39	### 2f. Pre-existing test coverage gaps
40	The current test suite does not yet pin the cameraman-specific override branch or the task-registry dedup branch. T1 confirmed that current collected cameraman coverage is limited to `test_parse_ltx_control_cameraman` and `test_needs_ic_lora[cameraman-True]`, and neither one reaches `get_ic_lora_entry()`.
41	
42	That leaves the cameraman override branch at `source/core/params/travel_guidance.py:310-313` and the dedup logic at `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:964-968` unexercised by current tests. Sprint 3 therefore needs new first-class test deliverables rather than relying on `-k` selectors over the existing suite.
43	
44	### 2g. Stale-doc inventory
45	T1 identified existing documentation that still describes `Wan2GP/` as a flat vendored tree prior to Sprint 2. Confirmed narrow-sweep hits are `README.md:39`, `STRUCTURE.md:22`, and `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202`.
46	
47	The broader sweep also found `STRUCTURE.md:70`, which still describes `Wan2GP/` as “Upstream video generation engine (vendored, do-not-edit-in-place).” Sprint 2 therefore needs a doc-sweep gate that covers both the narrow regex and the broader `Wan2GP` plus `vendor` pass, followed by manual reviewer confirmation because the regex is heuristic.
48	
49	### 2h. Invocation contract
50	The repo’s test and worker invocation contract is `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …`, consistent with the locked Python 3.10 environment in `uv.lock` and the worker run examples in `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:195`. This document should treat that as the canonical pytest invocation form for every later verification gate.
51	
52	Plain `pytest` is not sufficient in this repo because it can fail with `ModuleNotFoundError: source` when the expected environment contract is not active. The migration plan therefore needs to standardize on the `uv run --python 3.10` prefix anywhere it names pytest.
53	
54	### 2i. Path-contract verification technique
55	The path contract has two caller classes that must both survive the migration. First, `ensure_wan2gp_on_path()` inserts the absolute `Wan2GP/` directory itself into `sys.path`, not the repo root. Second, the more common dotted `Wan2GP.*` callers can work when either the repo root or `Wan2GP/` is on `sys.path`, so they are necessary but not sufficient proof that the mount contract remains correct.
56	
57	The stricter contract is driven by the bare-module callers in `vendor_imports.py:76-88`: `models.qwen.qwen_handler`, `shared.utils.loras_mutipliers`, and `models.qwen.qwen_main`. Those class-b callers require the absolute `Wan2GP/` insertion specifically, which is why the Sprint 2 smoke must assert `os.path.abspath(str(get_wan2gp_path())) in [os.path.abspath(p) for p in sys.path]` with no repo-root fallback.
58	
59	Bare-import reachability should be verified only through filesystem proxies such as `os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_handler.py')`, `os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_main.py')`, and `os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/shared/utils/loras_mutipliers.py')`. `find_spec` and real imports on `shared.utils.*` are intentionally avoided because `Wan2GP/shared/utils/__init__.py` eagerly imports SciPy-sensitive solver modules. Full end-to-end validation of the bare-module callers should then come from the existing test suite exercising `vendor_imports.py` through normal codepaths.
60	
61	## 3. Fork Architecture Decisions
62	The migration will use the hybrid architecture defined by SD-001: `banodoco/Wan2GP` becomes the long-running fork, and `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` becomes a git submodule pinned to that fork rather than a flat copied tree. That choice keeps the existing filesystem contract stable while making future rebases, drift review, and upstream comparison mechanically possible again.
63	
64	Patch ownership is split deliberately rather than informally. SD-002 defines the required triage buckets so every deviation is classified as accept-upstream, upstreamable, banodoco-fork-or-banodoco-patch, or cruft; SD-003 stages the resync by subsystem so low-risk `shared/` and `postprocessing/` work lands before the hot `models/ltx2/` zone; and SD-004 keeps LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion out of the migration path so the migration is not coupled to a defaults experiment.
65	
66	Feature exposure is also staged. SD-005 keeps `res_2s` and `self_refiner` behind off-by-default flags until the fork relationship is stable, and SD-006 keeps any post-gen detailer work outside this initiative so the migration remains focused on source-of-truth and drift control instead of product expansion.
67	
68	Sustainability is handled as part of the architecture, not as follow-up cleanup. SD-007 sets a monthly rebase cadence with event-triggered syncs on upstream minor-version bumps, while SD-008 adds scheduled drift detection in CI so the repository does not slide back into another opaque vendor snapshot. Together, SD-001 through SD-008 define a maintainable fork-plus-patch-layer model rather than a one-time resync.
69	
70	## 4. Sprint Plan (4 × 2 weeks)
71	### Sprint 1 — Triage & Fork Cut (Weeks 1-2)
72	#### Goals
73	- Fork `banodoco/Wan2GP` at upstream HEAD and establish it as the future source of truth.
74	- Build `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` and classify every differing file into the SD-002 buckets.
75	- Pre-classify `clear_conditioning` as bucket `(c)` before any hot-zone resync begins.
76	
77	#### Deliverables
78	- `banodoco/Wan2GP` fork created and available for migration work.
79	- `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` with rows covering the 22-file `models/ltx2/` hot zone and every differing file elsewhere.
80	
81	#### Verification Gates
82	- `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` contains at least one row for every differing file and explicit bucket decisions for all 22 `models/ltx2/` files.
83	- The `clear_conditioning` row remains pre-classified as bucket `(c)` with an explicit carry location and verification entry.
84	
85	#### Rollback Plan
86	- Record tag `wan2gp-mig-sprint-1-baseline` before opening the fork workstream.
87	- No `reigh-worker` code changes are planned in this sprint; if the fork cut is rejected, abandon the fork workstream and leave `reigh-worker` at `wan2gp-mig-sprint-1-baseline`.
88

90	- Zero `reigh-worker` code changes means zero risk to IC-LoRA behavior, `ltx_anchor`, `ltx_control`, `ltx_hybrid`, `clear_conditioning`, and the path/import contract during Sprint 1.
91	- The only outputs are the fork setup and the triage artifact, so every protected behavior is preserved by construction.

250	## 9. Settled Decisions
251	### Decision 1
252	- `id`: `SD-001`
253	- `load_bearing`: `true`
254	- `decision`: Use a hybrid fork shape: maintain `banodoco/Wan2GP` as the long-running fork and consume it in `reigh-worker` as a git submodule mounted at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`.
255	  Path/import contract sub-clause:
256	  - The submodule mount path stays exactly `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`; no relocation or rename.
257	  - `ensure_wan2gp_on_path()` must continue inserting the absolute `Wan2GP/` directory itself into `sys.path`, not the repo root or a parent/sibling path.
258	  - The bare-module callers in `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py:76-88` must remain valid, explicitly including `models.qwen.qwen_handler`, `shared.utils.loras_mutipliers`, and `models.qwen.qwen_main`.
259	  - The dotted `Wan2GP.*` callers remain supported as the other caller class.
260	  - Deviations that do not belong in the fork stay in the existing `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py` seam, which is module-loaded via `source/__init__.py:6` and applied by `apply_all_wgp_patches(wgp, self.wan_root)` at `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266`.
261	  - No parallel runtime-side Wan2GP patch package is introduced.
262	- `rationale`: This preserves the current runtime contract while restoring maintainability. The stable mount path protects the import surface already used by both dotted `Wan2GP.*` imports and the bare-module import sites, and the existing patch seam prevents fork-only changes from being scattered into a second, conflicting patch registry.
263	
264	### Decision 2
265	- `id`: `SD-002`
266	- `load_bearing`: `true`
267	- `decision`: Every deviation between the current vendored tree and upstream is triaged into one of four buckets: `(a) accept-upstream`, `(b) upstreamable`, `(c) banodoco-fork-or-banodoco-patch`, `(d) cruft`. The canonical artifact is `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` with columns `path,hunk,bucket,carry_location,owner,verification`.
268	  Illustrative sample row:
269	
270	  ```csv
271	  path,hunk,bucket,carry_location,owner,verification
272	  Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py,"clear_conditioning() strips appended keyframe tokens before VAE decode",banodoco-fork-or-banodoco-patch,banodoco/Wan2GP or source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py,worker-runtime,byte-compare saved latent fixture
273	  ```
274	- `rationale`: The migration risk is concentrated in drift classification, not in git mechanics alone. A forced bucket per hunk makes carry decisions reviewable, gives Sprint 1 a concrete deliverable, and prevents load-bearing customizations like `clear_conditioning` from being lost inside an undifferentiated merge.
275	
276	### Decision 3
277	- `id`: `SD-003`
278	- `load_bearing`: `true`
279	- `decision`: Resync by subsystem, not all at once: `shared/` plus `postprocessing/` first, `models/ltx2/` second, and defaults/configs last.
280	- `rationale`: The repo already depends on fragile `models/ltx2/` behavior, including `ltx_anchor`, IC-LoRA handling, and `clear_conditioning`. Staging the migration lowers blast radius, lets the team validate path/import and doc updates before touching the 22-file hot zone, and keeps rollback boundaries understandable.
281	
282	### Decision 4
283	- `id`: `SD-004`
284	- `load_bearing`: `false`
285	- `decision`: Hold LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion for a separate A/B sprint outside the migration, even if `ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` is imported during the resync.
286	- `rationale`: Default promotion is a product-choice experiment, not a prerequisite for establishing the fork relationship. Separating it from the migration keeps “source sync” and “new default behavior” from failing together or obscuring regressions.
287	
288	### Decision 5
289	- `id`: `SD-005`
290	- `load_bearing`: `false`
291	- `decision`: Introduce `res_2s` and refreshed `self_refiner` support behind off-by-default flags during Sprints 2 and 3, with any default promotion deferred until upstream behavior and local verification converge.
292	- `rationale`: Both features are desirable, but neither is required to complete the vendor-to-fork transition safely. Flagging them keeps the migration forward-moving without forcing immediate behavior changes in the travel pipeline.
293	
294	### Decision 6
295	- `id`: `SD-006`
296	- `load_bearing`: `false`
297	- `decision`: Keep post-gen detailer work out of this migration plan and treat it as a separate initiative after the fork-plus-patch relationship is stable.
298	- `rationale`: The user preference already points toward a task-handler implementation rather than inline travel-pipeline detailer logic. Folding that feature into the migration would expand scope, confuse verification ownership, and make it harder to distinguish migration regressions from brand-new behavior.
299	
300	### Decision 7
301	- `id`: `SD-007`
302	- `load_bearing`: `true`
303	- `decision`: Rebase the fork monthly, with additional event-triggered syncs whenever upstream ships a new minor version that touches relevant subsystems.
304	- `rationale`: Weekly rebases would create churn without matching the repo’s current staffing and risk profile, while ad hoc syncs are what created the current opaque drift. Monthly cadence plus event triggers is frequent enough to keep diffs reviewable and infrequent enough to be realistic.
305	
306	### Decision 8
307	- `id`: `SD-008`
308	- `load_bearing`: `true`
309	- `decision`: Add `.github/workflows/wan2gp-drift.yml` as a weekly scheduled CI job that compares the pinned submodule pointer against upstream HEAD and reports drift for review.
310	- `rationale`: Once the vendor tree becomes a submodule, drift becomes measurable. A scheduled CI check converts that measurability into an operating habit, so the repo does not return to a silent `.git`-less copy that is only audited after major divergence has already accumulated.
311

**clear_conditioning Function:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-worker/Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79`
```python
def clear_conditioning(self, latent_state: LatentState) -> LatentState:
    """
    Clear the conditioning from the latent state. This method removes extra tokens from the end of the latent.
    Therefore, conditioning items should add extra tokens ONLY to the end of the latent.
    """
    latent_state = latent_state.clone()

    num_tokens = self.patchifier.get_token_count(self.target_shape)
    latent = latent_state.latent[:, :num_tokens]
    clean_latent = latent_state.clean_latent[:, :num_tokens]
    denoise_mask = torch.ones_like(latent_state.denoise_mask)[:, :num_tokens]
    positions = latent_state.positions[:, :, :num_tokens]

    return LatentState(latent=latent, denoise_mask=denoise_mask, positions=positions, clean_latent=clean_latent)
```

**Defaults:** `ltx2_22B_distilled.json` exists at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-worker/Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2_22B_distilled.json`. **Missing:** `ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` (planned addition per Sprint 3 spec, not yet present).

# Implementation Plan: Sprint 3 — `models/ltx2/` Resync + Cameraman/Dedup Tests

## Overview
Execute Sprint 3 of the Wan2GP fork migration (`docs/WAN2GP_FORK_MIGRATION_PLAN.md:125-161`). The sprint has three work streams:

1. **Submodule hot-zone resync** — advance `Wan2GP/` (already a submodule pinned to `reigh-sprint-2`, per `.gitmodules`) to a new `reigh-sprint-3` branch on `banodoco/Wan2GP` that absorbs upstream `models/ltx2/` drift while carrying `clear_conditioning` (at `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79`) byte-identical.
2. **Defaults addition** — add `Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` as *available but not default* per SD-004.
3. **First-class test deliverables** — write `tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py` (cameraman override + pose fallback) and `tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py` (update-on-match + append-on-miss) that pin the currently-uncovered branches at `source/core/params/travel_guidance.py:305-325` and `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-971`.

**Sandbox constraints learned in Sprint 2 (must respect):**
- Executor **cannot** write `.git/` or push to remotes. Submodule bumps, tag creation (`wan2gp-mig-sprint-3-baseline`), and any remote-branch activity must be handed to the outer orchestrator via `megaplan override add-note`.
- `uv sync` fails on darwin-arm64 (decord==0.6.0 wheel gap). For any smoke that boots the bridge, build a minimal venv at `/tmp/sprint3-smoke-venv` using the Sprint 2 package set: `gradio torch diffusers transformers mmgp scipy Pillow numpy torchvision opencv-python imageio rembg onnxruntime ffmpeg-python`. Pure-pytest runs of the 6-file travel suite + new tests do **not** need this venv — they use `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …` per `docs/WAN2GP_FORK_MIGRATION_PLAN.md:50-52`.
- Do **not** commit the final reigh-worker work. Leave staged for user review (same as Sprint 2).

**Current baseline (from repo probe):**
- `.gitmodules` lists `Wan2GP/` with branch `reigh-sprint-2`, URL `https://github.com/banodoco/Wan2GP.git`.
- `clear_conditioning` is at `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79`, 14 lines, signature `clear_conditioning(self, latent_state: LatentState) -> LatentState`.
- `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE` and `_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL` are at `source/core/params/travel_guidance.py:29-37`; `get_ic_lora_entry()` at lines 305-325; `TravelGuidanceConfig` has `kind`, `mode`, `strength`, `control_strength`, `videos` fields, and `from_payload()` entry point (used in existing tests).
- Dedup branch is inlined at `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:962-970` (no standalone helper exists).
- No prior `wan2gp-mig-*` tags exist. No saved latent fixture exists under `tests/` or `fixtures/`.
- `ltx2_22B_distilled.json` exists at `Wan2GP/defaults/`; `ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` does **not**.
- Existing tests import via `from source.core.params.travel_guidance import TravelGuidanceConfig` and use `TravelGuidanceConfig.from_payload(payload, model_name)` (`tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py:10-11,41-56`).
- `scripts/live_test/` is the Sprint 2 smoke harness (currently untracked) — not required for Sprint 3 gates, but available for follow-up smoke if needed.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Establish Sprint 3 baseline & orchestrator handoff (read-only setup)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Record** the pre-sprint baseline by emitting a `megaplan override add-note` with the exact command for the orchestrator to run on its side: `git tag wan2gp-mig-sprint-3-baseline` (from reigh-worker root, at current HEAD). Note the same handoff for the submodule: orchestrator must, on `banodoco/Wan2GP`, create branch `reigh-sprint-3` based on the Sprint 2 tip and then advance it by merging/rebasing the selected upstream `models/ltx2/` drift (bucket `(a)` rows from `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv`) while preserving `clear_conditioning` byte-for-byte.
2. **Freeze** the current `clear_conditioning` reference. Read `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79` and save its exact bytes to a local file `scripts/sprint3/clear_conditioning_reference.py.txt` (executor-writable; not committed). This file is the pre-resync oracle for Gate 3.
3. **Freeze** `Wan2GP` submodule SHA now (from the existing checkout) as `PRE_RESYNC_SHA`. Capture in the executor's notes so the post-resync byte-compare can verify that the hot-zone resync did not alter `clear_conditioning`.

### Step 2: Capture `clear_conditioning` latent fixture oracle (`scripts/sprint3/`, `tests/fixtures/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Write** `scripts/sprint3/capture_clear_conditioning_fixture.py` — a minimal, deterministic harness that constructs a tiny synthetic `LatentState` (fixed seed, fixed shape, e.g. `latent` shape `(1, 64, 8)`, matching `denoise_mask`, `positions`, `clean_latent`) and a stub object exposing `patchifier.get_token_count()` returning a fixed `num_tokens` smaller than the token dim, calls `clear_conditioning` bound to that stub, and writes the output tensors via `torch.save({...}, "tests/fixtures/clear_conditioning_oracle.pt")`.
   - Rationale: "byte-compare against a saved latent fixture" (plan line 142) needs a reproducible input *and* expected output. The fixture file captures expected output against the **pre-resync** implementation.
2. **Create** `tests/fixtures/` if absent, write `clear_conditioning_oracle.pt`, and add a short README note inline (or a top-of-file docstring in the capture script) pointing to how to regenerate.
3. **Add** `tests/test_clear_conditioning_byte_identity.py` that loads the fixture and re-runs the current `clear_conditioning` implementation with the same synthetic input, then asserts the output tensors match byte-for-byte (`torch.equal` on each field, or raw `.numpy().tobytes()` compare). This test is the **post-resync** byte-compare gate — it runs green now (pre-resync), then again after Step 5 without any change.

### Step 3: Write `tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py`
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py` importing `from source.core.params.travel_guidance import TravelGuidanceConfig` (same pattern as `tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py:11`).
2. **Add** `test_get_ic_lora_entry_cameraman_returns_cseti_url`: build `TravelGuidanceConfig(kind="ltx_control", mode="cameraman", strength=0.7, videos=[{"path": "/tmp/g.mp4", "start_frame": 0, "end_frame": 16, "treatment": "adjust"}])` (or use `from_payload` for parity with existing tests), call `get_ic_lora_entry()`, and assert:
   - `entry["path"] == "https://huggingface.co/Cseti/LTX2.3-22B_IC-LoRA-Cameraman_v1/resolve/main/LTX2.3-22B_IC-LoRA-Cameraman_v1_10500.safetensors"`
   - `entry["name"] == "ic-lora-cameraman (auto-injected)"`
   - `entry["strength"] == 0.7` (matches the `strength` field, because `kind="ltx_control"` uses `self.strength` per `travel_guidance.py:316-318`).
3. **Add** `test_get_ic_lora_entry_pose_falls_back_to_union_control`: build a config with `kind="ltx_control"`, `mode="pose"`, and assert `entry["path"]` equals the `_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL` constant value (`"ltx-2.3-22b-ic-lora-union-control-ref0.5.safetensors"`). Import the constant via `from source.core.params.travel_guidance import _IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL` so the assertion stays in sync with the registry.
4. **Do not** use `pytest -k` selectors (plan line 147 forbids them).

### Step 4: Extract dedup helper + write `tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py` (`source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py`, `tests/`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Refactor** the inlined dedup at `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:961-970` into a small module-level helper in the same file, e.g.:
   ```python
   def _inject_ic_lora_with_dedup(segment_loras: list[dict], ic_entry: dict) -> list[dict]:
       segment_loras = list(segment_loras or [])
       ic_basename = os.path.basename(ic_entry["path"])
       for existing in segment_loras:
           if os.path.basename(existing.get("path", "")) == ic_basename:
               existing["strength"] = ic_entry["strength"]
               return segment_loras
       segment_loras.append(ic_entry)
       return segment_loras
   ```
   Replace the inline block with `segment_loras = _inject_ic_lora_with_dedup(segment_loras, ic_entry)`. Keep the existing debug-log line. No behavioral change.
2. **Create** `tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py` importing `from source.task_handlers.tasks.task_registry import _inject_ic_lora_with_dedup`.
3. **Add** `test_dedup_updates_strength_when_basename_matches`: seed `segment_loras=[{"path": "https://example.com/ltx-2.3-22b-ic-lora-union-control-ref0.5.safetensors", "strength": 0.4}]`, call helper with `ic_entry={"path": "ltx-2.3-22b-ic-lora-union-control-ref0.5.safetensors", "strength": 0.8, "name": "ic-lora-pose (auto-injected)"}`, assert `len(result) == 1` and `result[0]["strength"] == 0.8`.
4. **Add** `test_dedup_appends_when_no_match`: seed empty list, inject any entry, assert `len(result) == 1` and `result[0] == ic_entry` (single appearance).

### Step 5: Resync `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/` against `banodoco/Wan2GP reigh-sprint-3`
**Scope:** Medium (orchestrator-gated write)
1. **Handoff to orchestrator** via `megaplan override add-note`: the submodule bump (updating `Wan2GP` working tree + `.gitmodules` branch pointer to `reigh-sprint-3`) requires `.git/` writes the executor cannot perform. Provide the orchestrator with:
   - Target branch: `reigh-sprint-3` on `banodoco/Wan2GP`.
   - Invariant: `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py` lines for `clear_conditioning` must remain byte-identical to the frozen reference saved in Step 1.
   - Bucket filter: only upstream drift classified `(a) accept-upstream` in `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` for files under `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/`.
2. **After orchestrator advances the submodule**, executor re-runs the byte-identity test (`tests/test_clear_conditioning_byte_identity.py`) and the 6-file travel suite. If the byte-compare fails, raise this immediately as a blocker — the resync must not alter `clear_conditioning`.

### Step 6: Add `Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` (available, not default)
**Scope:** Small (orchestrator-gated — file lives inside submodule working tree, which requires a commit *inside* the submodule)
1. **Source** the file content from upstream banodoco `reigh-sprint-3` branch (per SD-004 — imported during resync but not promoted to default). If upstream already carries it, the Step 5 resync lands it automatically. If not, hand the content to the orchestrator with instructions: "commit `defaults/ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` on `banodoco/Wan2GP reigh-sprint-3` and re-bump the submodule".
2. **Verify** no default-selection code in `source/` references the new filename (grep for `ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1` under `source/` — must be zero hits). SD-004 mandates that default promotion is deferred to a separate A/B sprint.

### Step 7: Run all Sprint 3 verification gates
**Scope:** Small
1. **New IC-LoRA tests**: `uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py`
2. **Full 6-file travel suite**: `uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py`
3. **`clear_conditioning` byte-compare**: `uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_clear_conditioning_byte_identity.py`
4. **PATH A skip gate** (already inside the 6-file suite, re-invoke for the gate artifact): `uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py`
5. **20 anchor tests**: `uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py` — confirm total anchor-related test count is ≥ 20 (the plan names "20 anchor tests" as an explicit expected count in line 145–146).
6. **No `-k` selectors**: verify by inspection; do not add any `-k cameraman` or `-k "ic_lora and cameraman"` invocations anywhere (plan line 147).

### Step 8: Stage changes for user review, write handoff notes
**Scope:** Small
1. **`git status`** — confirm changed tree contains only:
   - `tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py` (new)
   - `tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py` (new)
   - `tests/test_clear_conditioning_byte_identity.py` (new)
   - `tests/fixtures/clear_conditioning_oracle.pt` (new)
   - `scripts/sprint3/capture_clear_conditioning_fixture.py` (new)
   - `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py` (small edit: extracted `_inject_ic_lora_with_dedup` helper)
   - Submodule pointer bump for `Wan2GP/` (if the orchestrator has already advanced the submodule)
   - Possibly `Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` (if Step 6 required out-of-band copy; otherwise landed via submodule bump)
2. **`git add -A` but do NOT commit** — leave staged for the user (same rule as Sprint 2).
3. **Emit `megaplan override add-note`** summarizing:
   - Pending orchestrator actions: create `wan2gp-mig-sprint-3-baseline` tag at pre-sprint HEAD, create and advance `banodoco/Wan2GP` branch `reigh-sprint-3`, bump submodule pointer, (optional) push.
   - All executor-side gates run and their pass/fail status.
   - Any anomalies (e.g., if upstream `models/ltx2/` drift touched `clear_conditioning` — in which case the resync must back out those hunks).

## Execution Order
1. Steps 1–4 first (pure executor-side work, no submodule mutations): baseline capture, fixture, new tests, dedup helper extraction.
2. Steps 5–6 next (orchestrator-gated submodule mutations).
3. Steps 7–8 last (verification + staging).

## Validation Order
1. Run `tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py` and `tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py` first — cheapest, pins the two new branches against the **current** code before any resync churn.
2. Run `tests/test_clear_conditioning_byte_identity.py` **before** submodule bump (must pass — confirms the fixture oracle matches pre-resync behavior).
3. Run the full 6-file travel suite pre-resync as a sanity gate.
4. (Orchestrator advances submodule.)
5. Re-run `tests/test_clear_conditioning_byte_identity.py` **after** submodule bump — must still pass byte-for-byte.
6. Re-run the full 6-file travel suite post-resync.
7. Re-run the new IC-LoRA and dedup tests post-resync.
8. Confirm anchor test count ≥ 20 via pytest collection output on `tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py`.

        - Before addressing individual flags, check: does any flag suggest the plan is targeting the wrong code or the wrong root cause? If so, consider whether the plan needs a new approach rather than adjustments. Explain your reasoning.

        - Preserve or improve success criteria quality. Each criterion must have a `priority` of `must`, `should`, or `info`. Promote or demote priorities if critique feedback reveals a criterion was over- or under-weighted.
        - Verify that the plan remains aligned with the user's original intent, not just internal plan quality.
        - Remove unjustified scope growth. If critique raised scope creep, narrow the plan back to the original idea unless the broader work is strictly required.

        Plan template — simple format (adapt to the actual repo and scope):
````md
# Implementation Plan: [Title]

## Overview
Summarize the goal, current repository shape, and the constraints that matter.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit the current behavior (`megaplan/prompts.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Inspect** the current implementation and call out the exact insertion points (`megaplan/prompts.py:29`).

### Step 2: Add the first change (`megaplan/evaluation.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** the smallest viable change with exact file references (`megaplan/evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Capture** any tricky behavior with a short example.
   ```python
   issues = validate_plan_structure(plan_text)
   ```

### Step 3: Wire downstream behavior (`megaplan/handlers.py`, `megaplan/workers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** the runtime flow in the touched files (`megaplan/handlers.py:400`, `megaplan/workers.py:199`).

### Step 4: Prove the change (`tests/test_evaluation.py`, `tests/test_megaplan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the cheapest targeted checks first (`tests/test_evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Finish** with broader verification once the wiring is in place (`tests/test_megaplan.py:1`).

## Execution Order
1. Update prompts and mocks before enforcing stricter validation.
2. Land higher-risk wiring after the validator and tests are ready.

## Validation Order
1. Start with focused unit tests.
2. Run the broader suite after the flow changes are in place.
````

For complex plans, use multiple phases:
````md
## Phase 1: Foundation — Dependencies, DB, Types

### Step 1: Install dependencies (`package.json`)
...

### Step 2: Create database migration (`supabase/migrations/`)
...

## Phase 2: Core Integration

### Step 3: Port the main component (`src/components/`)
...
````

Template guidance:
- Simple plans: use `## Main Phase` with `### Step N:` sections underneath.
- Complex plans: use multiple `## Phase N:` sections, each containing `### Step N:` steps. Step numbers are global (not per-phase).
- The flat `## Step N:` format (without phases) also works for backwards compatibility.
- Key invariants: one H1 title, one `## Overview`, numbered step sections (`### Step N:` or `## Step N:`), and at least one ordering section.

# Implementation Plan: Sprint 3 — `models/ltx2/` Resync + Cameraman/Dedup Tests (Revision 2)

## Overview
Execute Sprint 3 of the Wan2GP fork migration (`docs/WAN2GP_FORK_MIGRATION_PLAN.md:125-161`). Three work streams:

1. **Submodule hot-zone resync** — advance `Wan2GP/` (currently a submodule whose `.gitmodules` entry names branch `reigh-sprint-2` but is in a detached-HEAD checkout) to a pinned SHA on `banodoco/Wan2GP reigh-sprint-3`. Scope covers bucket-`(a)` triage rows under `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/` **and** the `Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` row, while carrying `clear_conditioning` (at `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79`) byte-identical.
2. **Defaults addition** — ensure `Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` lands in the `reigh-sprint-3` branch and is discoverable, but not referenced as default (per SD-004).
3. **First-class test deliverables** — write `tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py` and `tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py` to pin the currently-uncovered branches at `source/core/params/travel_guidance.py:305-325` and `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-971`.

**Sandbox constraints (Sprint 2 lessons, restated precisely):**
- Executor **cannot** write anything under `.git/` (including `.git/index`). That means **no `git add`, no `git commit`, no `git tag`, no `git push`, no submodule pointer bump**. All such operations are orchestrator-gated and handed off via `megaplan override add-note`.
- `uv sync` fails on darwin-arm64 (decord==0.6.0 wheel gap). Build `/tmp/sprint3-smoke-venv` on-demand using Sprint 2's package set (`gradio torch diffusers transformers mmgp scipy Pillow numpy torchvision opencv-python imageio rembg onnxruntime ffmpeg-python`). Only the path-contract smoke (`python -c` one-liner) needs this venv; the pure-pytest gates run under `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …`.
- Executor leaves working-tree edits in place (unstaged) and produces a machine-readable file list + patch bundle for the orchestrator to stage.

**Current baseline (re-probed):**
- `.gitmodules` names branch `reigh-sprint-2`; `Wan2GP/` checkout is **detached HEAD** — so `git -C Wan2GP rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD` returns `HEAD`. Submodule validation must be **SHA-based**, not name-based.
- `clear_conditioning` at `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79`, 14 lines.
- `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE`/`_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL` at `source/core/params/travel_guidance.py:29-37`; `get_ic_lora_entry()` at 305-325.
- Dedup inlined at `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:962-970`.
- `ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` does **not** exist under `Wan2GP/defaults/` today.
- Sole caller of `get_ic_lora_entry()` is `task_registry.py:958`; its return value flows into `activated_loras`/`loras_multipliers` via the same block.
- `clear_conditioning` live callers: `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py:1257,1259,1276`.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Capture pre-resync state & emit orchestrator handoff #1 (read-only)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Snapshot** the current `Wan2GP` submodule SHA via `git -C Wan2GP rev-parse HEAD` (read-only) and the content of `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79` into an executor-local scratch file at `scripts/sprint3/_pre_resync_notes.txt` (inside the working tree, not committed). This captures `PRE_RESYNC_SHA` and the `clear_conditioning` source bytes as pre-resync oracles.
2. **Emit handoff note #1** via `megaplan override add-note` listing orchestrator tasks that the executor cannot perform:

Invariant: `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py` `clear_conditioning` bytes must equal the pre-resync reference captured in §1.
   - Update `.gitmodules` branch entry from `reigh-sprint-2` → `reigh-sprint-3` and bump the `Wan2GP/` submodule pointer to the new remote tip.

### Step 2: Capture `clear_conditioning` latent fixture oracle (`scripts/sprint3/`, `tests/fixtures/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Write** `scripts/sprint3/capture_clear_conditioning_fixture.py` — deterministic (fixed seed, CPU-only, no network, no GPU). Construct a synthetic `LatentState` and a stub with `patchifier.get_token_count()` returning a fixed `num_tokens` smaller than the token dim. Call `clear_conditioning` bound to the stub. `torch.save({"inputs": {...}, "outputs": {...}}, "tests/fixtures/clear_conditioning_oracle.pt")`.
2. **Create** `tests/fixtures/` if absent; write `clear_conditioning_oracle.pt`.
3. **Add** `tests/test_clear_conditioning_byte_identity.py` that loads the fixture, re-runs `clear_conditioning` with the recorded inputs, and asserts each output tensor (`latent`, `clean_latent`, `denoise_mask`, `positions`) is byte-equal (`torch.equal` + shape check, plus `.numpy().tobytes() == ...` for the tensor buffers). This single-shape probe is intentionally narrower than the three live caller sites in `helpers.py:1257,1259,1276` — see §"Known narrowings" below.

### Step 3: Write `tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py`
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** the file. Import `from source.core.params.travel_guidance import TravelGuidanceConfig, _IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL`.
2. **`test_get_ic_lora_entry_cameraman_returns_cseti_url`**: construct `TravelGuidanceConfig(kind="ltx_control", mode="cameraman", strength=0.7, videos=[VIDEO_ENTRY])` (matching the existing `tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py:14-19` fixture pattern); call `get_ic_lora_entry()`; assert:

   - `entry["name"] == "ic-lora-cameraman (auto-injected)"`
   - `entry["strength"] == 0.7` (`travel_guidance.py:316-318` uses `self.strength` for `ltx_control`).
3. **`test_get_ic_lora_entry_pose_falls_back_to_union_control`**: `mode="pose"`, assert `entry["path"] == _IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL`.
4. **No `-k` selectors** anywhere (plan line 147).

### Step 4: Extract dedup helper + write `tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py`
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Refactor** `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:961-970`. Extract to module level:
   ```python
   def _inject_ic_lora_with_dedup(segment_loras, ic_entry):
       segment_loras = list(segment_loras or [])
       ic_basename = os.path.basename(ic_entry["path"])
       for existing in segment_loras:
           if os.path.basename(existing.get("path", "")) == ic_basename:
               existing["strength"] = ic_entry["strength"]
               return segment_loras
       segment_loras.append(ic_entry)
       return segment_loras
   ```
   Replace the inline block with `segment_loras = _inject_ic_lora_with_dedup(segment_loras, ic_entry)`. Preserve the existing `headless_logger.debug(...)` line. No behavioral change.
2. **Create** `tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py` importing `from source.task_handlers.tasks.task_registry import _inject_ic_lora_with_dedup`.
3. **`test_dedup_updates_strength_when_basename_matches`**: seed `[{"path": "https://example.com/ltx-2.3-22b-ic-lora-union-control-ref0.5.safetensors", "strength": 0.4}]`; inject `{"path": "ltx-2.3-22b-ic-lora-union-control-ref0.5.safetensors", "strength": 0.8, "name": "ic-lora-pose (auto-injected)"}`; assert `len == 1` and `strength == 0.8`.
4. **`test_dedup_appends_when_no_match`**: seed empty; inject any entry; assert `len == 1` and item equals injected entry.
5. **Integration coverage delegated**: the downstream wiring of `get_ic_lora_entry()` → `activated_loras`/`loras_multipliers` (`task_registry.py:973-987`) is already covered by the 6-file travel suite's `_build_generation_params` paths. This is an explicit narrowing (see callers-1 acceptance below).

### Step 6: Verify `Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` is discoverable but not default
**Scope:** Small
1. **File presence**: `pathlib.Path([REDACTED]).is_file()` returns True.
2. **JSON loadability**: `json.loads(Path([REDACTED]).read_text())` parses to a non-empty dict. This addresses the `all_locations` flag — a malformed or misnamed file would pass a "file exists" check but fail Wan2GP's `defaults/*.json` glob loader at `Wan2GP/wgp.py:2970-2992`.
3. **Discovery probe**: inside `/tmp/sprint3-smoke-venv`, run:
   ```bash
   python -c "from source.core.runtime_paths import ensure_wan2gp_on_path; ensure_wan2gp_on_path(); from source.runtime.wgp_bridge import get_model_def; d = get_model_def('ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1'); assert isinstance(d, dict) and d; print('discovery OK')"
   ```
   If the venv is not available, fall back to a direct glob scan: `python -c "import glob, json; paths = glob.glob('Wan2GP/defaults/*.json'); assert [REDACTED] in paths; json.load(open([REDACTED]))"` and record the fallback in handoff note #2.
4. **Non-default assertion**: `grep -rn 'ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1' source/ Wan2GP/defaults/*.json` — zero hits under `source/`; references may exist only inside the new JSON file itself (self-reference is fine).

**Scope:** Small

   ```bash
   REMOTE_SHA=$(git ls-remote https://github.com/banodoco/Wan2GP.git reigh-sprint-3 | awk '{print $1}')
   LOCAL_SHA=$(git -C Wan2GP rev-parse HEAD)
   test -n "$REMOTE_SHA" && test "$LOCAL_SHA" = "$REMOTE_SHA" || echo "MISMATCH: local=$LOCAL_SHA remote=$REMOTE_SHA"
   git -C Wan2GP branch -r --contains "$LOCAL_SHA" | grep -q 'origin/reigh-sprint-3'
   ```
   Both assertions must hold. Also assert `.gitmodules` `[submodule "Wan2GP"] branch = reigh-sprint-3`.

**Scope:** Small
1. **Inventory** unstaged changes via read-only git commands (these do not write `.git/`):
   - `git status --porcelain -uall` — captures modified + untracked files.
   - `git diff` (working tree) — tracked-file edits.
2. **Produce artifacts for the orchestrator** inside the working tree (outside `.git/`):
   - `scripts/sprint3/_handoff/staged_file_list.txt` — one path per line, exactly the files the orchestrator should `git add`:
     - `tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py`
     - `tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py`
     - `tests/test_clear_conditioning_byte_identity.py`
     - `tests/fixtures/clear_conditioning_oracle.pt`
     - `scripts/sprint3/capture_clear_conditioning_fixture.py`
     - `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py` (dedup extraction)
     - `.gitmodules` (if branch entry updated, orchestrator-owned in Step 5)
     - `Wan2GP` (submodule pointer bump, orchestrator-owned in Step 5)
   - `scripts/sprint3/_handoff/working_tree.patch` — output of `git diff HEAD -- . ':(exclude)Wan2GP' ':(exclude).git'` piped to a file via Python `pathlib.Path.write_text()`, not shell redirection into `.git/`.
3. **Emit handoff note #2** via `megaplan override add-note`:
   - Files to stage (from list above).
   - All gate pass/fail results from Step 7.
   - Pre-resync SHA, post-resync SHA, and remote SHA (from Step 7.7).
   - Any anomalies (e.g., if `clear_conditioning` byte-compare failed, block the whole sprint).
4. **Do not run `git add`, `git commit`, `git tag`, or `git push`.** All of these write to `.git/` and are orchestrator-owned.

## Known narrowings (accepted tradeoffs — callers-1, callers-2)
- **callers-1 (IC-LoRA integration)**: The new unit tests pin value-level branches in `get_ic_lora_entry()` and `_inject_ic_lora_with_dedup()`. Downstream wiring into `activated_loras`/`loras_multipliers` (`task_registry.py:973-987`) is covered by the existing 6-file travel suite's `_build_generation_params` integration paths. Accepted: unit + existing integration coverage together pin the user-visible contract without adding a redundant integration test here.
- **callers-2 (`clear_conditioning` single-fixture probe)**: Live callers at `helpers.py:1257,1259,1276` invoke `clear_conditioning` with video, audio, and preview token shapes, but the function's implementation (`tools.py:66-79`) is a uniform slice operation with no shape-dependent branches. A single synthetic fixture exercises the full slicing logic. Accepted: probe is narrower than caller surface but is behaviorally complete for the function under test.

## Execution Order
1. Steps 1–4 (pure executor work, no submodule mutation): state capture, fixture, new tests, dedup helper extraction.
2. Step 5 (orchestrator-gated): submodule resync with widened scope (`models/ltx2/` **and** `defaults/ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json`).
3. Step 6 (executor-side verification of defaults JSON).
4. Step 7 (all verification gates including restored path-contract smoke and SHA-based submodule check).
5. Step 8 (orchestrator-gated staging handoff — executor produces file list + patch, does not `git add`).

## Validation Order
1. Pre-resync: Steps 2–4 tests pass against current code (Steps 2, 3, 4 together cover new IC-LoRA + dedup + byte-identity oracles).
2. Pre-resync: Full 6-file travel suite green.
3. Pre-resync: Strict path-contract smoke green (baseline, proves Sprint 2 state is intact).
4. Orchestrator advances submodule (Step 5).
5. Post-resync: `tests/test_clear_conditioning_byte_identity.py` still green (no drift in `clear_conditioning`).
6. Post-resync: Full 6-file travel suite still green.
7. Post-resync: New IC-LoRA + dedup tests still green.
8. Post-resync: Strict path-contract smoke still green.
9. Post-resync: Submodule SHA verification (local HEAD equals `origin/reigh-sprint-3` tip; `.gitmodules` names `reigh-sprint-3`).
10. Post-resync: Defaults JSON discoverable (Step 6).
11. Anchor test count ≥ 20 (total) and ≥ 12 (anchor-specific).
12. Grep for `-k` selector abuse — zero hits.

':(exclude)Wan2GP' ':(exclude).git'` misses all untracked adds (the five new test/fixture/script files). Since `git add -N` writes `.git/index`, use an alternative the executor *can* do without touching `.git/`: emit a tarball `scripts/sprint3/_handoff/unstaged_additions.tar` containing the untracked files, **plus** `working_tree.patch` for tracked-file edits (the `task_registry.py` dedup extraction) \u2014 or simply a per-file list that the orchestrator uses with `git add` itself. The staged_file_list.txt is fine; the patch shape must be reworked so the orchestrator receives the full file content, not just tracked modifications.\n\n4. **correctness-3 (non-default grep)** \u2014 The command `grep -rn 'ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1' source/ Wan2GP/defaults/*.json` legitimately matches the new JSON's own self-reference, so the assertion is not machine-checkable as written.         Plan template — simple format (adapt to the actual repo and scope):
````md
# Implementation Plan: [Title]

## Overview
Summarize the goal, current repository shape, and the constraints that matter.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit the current behavior (`megaplan/prompts.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Inspect** the current implementation and call out the exact insertion points (`megaplan/prompts.py:29`).

### Step 2: Add the first change (`megaplan/evaluation.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** the smallest viable change with exact file references (`megaplan/evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Capture** any tricky behavior with a short example.
   ```python
   issues = validate_plan_structure(plan_text)
   ```

### Step 3: Wire downstream behavior (`megaplan/handlers.py`, `megaplan/workers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** the runtime flow in the touched files (`megaplan/handlers.py:400`, `megaplan/workers.py:199`).

### Step 4: Prove the change (`tests/test_evaluation.py`, `tests/test_megaplan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the cheapest targeted checks first (`tests/test_evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Finish** with broader verification once the wiring is in place (`tests/test_megaplan.py:1`).

## Execution Order
1. Update prompts and mocks before enforcing stricter validation.
2. Land higher-risk wiring after the validator and tests are ready.

## Validation Order
1. Start with focused unit tests.
2. Run the broader suite after the flow changes are in place.
````

For complex plans, use multiple phases:
````md
## Phase 1: Foundation — Dependencies, DB, Types

### Step 1: Install dependencies (`package.json`)
...

### Step 2: Create database migration (`supabase/migrations/`)
...

## Phase 2: Core Integration

### Step 3: Port the main component (`src/components/`)
...
````

Template guidance:
- Simple plans: use `## Main Phase` with `### Step N:` sections underneath.
- Complex plans: use multiple `## Phase N:` sections, each containing `### Step N:` steps. Step numbers are global (not per-phase).
- The flat `## Step N:` format (without phases) also works for backwards compatibility.
- Key invariants: one H1 title, one `## Overview`, numbered step sections (`### Step N:` or `## Step N:`), and at least one ordering section.

Three work streams:\n\n1. **Submodule hot-zone resync** — advance `Wan2GP/` (submodule whose `.gitmodules` names `reigh-sprint-2` but sits in a detached-HEAD checkout) to a pinned SHA on `banodoco/Wan2GP reigh-sprint-3`. Scope: bucket-`(a)` triage rows under `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/` **and** `Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json`; `clear_conditioning` at `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79` stays byte-identical.\n2. **Defaults addition** — land `ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` as discoverable-but-not-default per SD-004.\n3. **First-class test deliverables** — `tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py` + `tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py` to pin the currently-uncovered branches at `source/core/params/travel_guidance.py:305-325` and `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-971`.\n\n**Sandbox constraints (precise):**\n- Executor **cannot write `.git/`** (index, refs, hooks, packed-refs, objects). That forbids `git add`, `git commit`, `git tag`, `git push`, submodule pointer bumps, and `.gitmodules` edits (the file itself is a working-tree text file the executor *can* read; Sprint 2 convention is the orchestrator owns the edit in concert with the submodule bump). All such ops are handed off via `megaplan override add-note`.\n- Executor **cannot reach external hosts** (sandbox DNS blocks github.com). Any gate that needs `git ls-remote` or network fetch is delegated to the orchestrator. Local-only checks stay with the executor.\n- `uv sync` fails on darwin-arm64 (decord==0.6.0).
